- read with inbox #14 (the icloud lane's review of the solved-exterior temperature, item 4): "the mass outside the back-averaged root" in the (a) lines above is computed as 1 - r_Hm/(2GE), r_Hm the applied-field root (the jsons' 1 - rHm_over_rs under the held-fixed record's group), the review's "root-deficit proxy": not an outside Gauss mass (that needs the geometric fields' enclosed mass) and not dust (that needs the source split); the comparison 1 - (9/4) sigma^2/r_s^2 is "a conditional vacuum comparison with its extension/tail errors", not an error bound for the matter-bearing applied profile.

### (6) The capture run's non-return fraction: not settled within 56 r_s under the in-transit gate (C-capture version 9 on the production's 2048-site chain, whose outer end has no absorber: that end returns weight from 56 to 58 r_s on in every probe, so the 300 r_s minimum run reads to 56 r_s and its later readings are not non-return fractions)

The comparator is the massive critical disk pi b_c^2(omega, m), 3.94, 2.13, 1.52 and 1.22 times the massless 27 pi G^2 E^2 at omega/m 1.1, 1.25, 1.5 and 2 (never the massless disk alone at these frequencies); the inward-classified weight is the positive-band group-velocity sign applied to both spin components, not a spinor-band projection or an integrated flux; sigma/(pi b_c^2) is a summed ratio, not a packet probability; the omega/m 2 probe is lattice-kinematic (its group speed 6.2 percent below the continuum's); the excluded launch channels and the cap at 200 are uncertified tails. At the last reading before the chain-end arrival (54 to 56 r_s) the ratio read 0.1994, 0.1257, 0.1045 and 0.0182 on the solved slice (0.2449, 0.1501, 0.1168 and 0.0192 at 40 r_s) with 0.65 to 0.76 of it in transit, and 0.2146, 0.1440, 0.1263 and 0.0228 on the exact-exterior control (0.57 to 0.68 in transit): unsettled, the chain and not the physics ending the reading. A version 10 run with an outer absorbing port (engine spec 38.5 (3)) replaces this row when it lands; the maximum's 300 r_s run was dropped.

- every number above is printed by the script; f_nr = A = 1 - R(T)/I with R(T) the whole outward-branch weight outside R_t + 2 at the reading time (block-wise Gabor projection; every core-mediated return by then included; no Pauli factor);

- C-capture version 9 to 300 r_s at the production's last minimum (T/rs 37.75; slice 302; tag ccap9t300_prod19_min), as engine_capture_read_v3 printed it (capture_read_v3_v9_t300_min_final.md, verbatim):

## C-capture version 9 read against the massive critical disk (engine_capture_read_v3.py; engine spec 37.6 (1) to (4))

- the comparator is pi b_c^2(omega, m), the massive critical disk (b_c from each run's own label), C_m = pi b_c^2(omega, m)/(27 pi (g E)^2) times the massless disk; sigma/(pi b_c^2) is a summed ratio, (pi/p^2) sum_kappa kappa [f(+kappa) + f(-kappa)] over that constant, not a packet probability.
- "inward-classified weight": the positive-band group-velocity sign applied to both spin components; not a spinor-band projection or an integrated flux (37.6 (1)); the runs' columns headed "inward-branch" carry this weight.
- uncertified tails: the launch channels the engine excluded (taken as A = 0), and the channels above the cap where a probe's channel list reaches it.
- lattice-kinematic: the probe's lattice group speed below the continuum sqrt(1 - 1/e^2) by more than 5 percent (the lead's threshold, 37.6 (2)).
- the chain-end arrival (the builder's reading aid, threshold 0.01 of pi b_c^2(omega, m)): the first reading time at which the plain norm beyond R_max = R_N - 16, summed as sigma, exceeds it; the chain's outer end has no absorber (the absorbing port is the inner one), so readings from there on carry weight that left the counted region outward; "none" means the threshold is not reached within the run.
